        selectionSpacingCells:spacingCells15841,
        selectionSpacingM:spacingCells15841*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1)),
        records:[],levels:[],summary:null
      };
    }
    const fate15841=diag15841.preselectionFate15841;
    const found15841=params15841.found||[];
    const actualOut15841=params15841.out||[];
    const replay15841=[];
    const idByCandidate15841=new Map();
    found15841.forEach((candidate15841,index15841)=>{
      idByCandidate15841.set(candidate15841,
        "P15841-L"+levelIndex15841+"-R"+(index15841+1));
    });
    for(let index15841=0;index15841<found15841.length;index15841++){
      const candidate15841=found15841[index15841];
      let selected15841=false,reason15841="below-seed-budget-cutoff",blocker15841=null;
      if(replay15841.length<maxSeeds15841){
        blocker15841=replay15841.find(seed15841=>
          Math.hypot(candidate15841.x-seed15841.x,candidate15841.y-seed15841.y)<=spacingCells15841
        )||null;
        if(blocker15841){
          reason15841="blocked-by-seed-spacing";
        }else{
          replay15841.push(candidate15841);
          selected15841=true;
          reason15841="selected";
        }
      }
      if(inside15841(candidate15841.x,candidate15841.y)){
        const record15841={
          selectionCandidateId:idByCandidate15841.get(candidate15841),
          levelIndex:levelIndex15841,
          elevation:Number(params15841.L),
          x:Number(candidate15841.x),y:Number(candidate15841.y),
          sampled:true,
          preselectionRank:index15841+1,
          flowCrossingValue:Number(candidate15841.flowCrossingValue||0),
          legacyQuality:Number(candidate15841.legacyQuality||0),
          selected:selected15841,
          terminalSelectionReason:reason15841
        };
        if(blocker15841){
          const distanceCells15841=Math.hypot(candidate15841.x-blocker15841.x,candidate15841.y-blocker15841.y);
          record15841.blockingSeedId=idByCandidate15841.get(blocker15841)||null;
          record15841.blockingSeedX=Number(blocker15841.x);
          record15841.blockingSeedY=Number(blocker15841.y);
          record15841.blockingDistanceCells=distanceCells15841;
          record15841.blockingDistanceM=distanceCells15841*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1));
        }
        fate15841.records.push(record15841);
      }
    }
    let notSampledTargetCount15841=0;
    const maxAcc15841=Number(params15841.maxAcc);
    const minorThr15841=Number(params15841.minorThr);
    const stride15841=Number(params15841.stride);
    const y015841=Math.max(2,Math.floor(disk15841.gy-disk15841.radiusCells));
    const y115841=Math.min(GH-3,Math.ceil(disk15841.gy+disk15841.radiusCells));
    const x015841=Math.max(2,Math.floor(disk15841.gx-disk15841.radiusCells));
    const x115841=Math.min(GW-3,Math.ceil(disk15841.gx+disk15841.radiusCells));
    for(let y15841=y015841;y15841<=y115841;y15841++)for(let x15841=x015841;x15841<=x115841;x15841++){
      if(!inside15841(x15841,y15841))continue;
      const sampledByStride15841=((y15841-2)%stride15841===0)&&((x15841-2)%stride15841===0);
      if(sampledByStride15841)continue;
      const i15841=idx(x15841,y15841);
      if(!M.inP[i15841]||M.pond[i15841]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i15841]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i15841]||0)>=params15841.chanThr||slopePct(x15841,y15841)>params15841.maxS)continue;
      if(!earthlineCellCrossesLevel(x15841,y15841,params15841.L))continue;
      const metrics15841=earthlineSeedMetricsAt15841(x15841,y15841,maxAcc15841,minorThr15841,params15841.chanThr);
      fate15841.records.push({
        selectionCandidateId:"P15841-L"+levelIndex15841+"-U"+x15841+"-"+y15841,
        levelIndex:levelIndex15841,
        elevation:Number(params15841.L),
        x:Number(x15841),y:Number(y15841),
        sampled:false,
        preselectionRank:null,
        flowCrossingValue:Number(metrics15841.flowCrossingValue||0),
        legacyQuality:Number(metrics15841.legacyQuality||0),
        selected:false,
        terminalSelectionReason:"not-sampled-by-stride"
      });
      notSampledTargetCount15841++;
    }
    const replayMatchesActual15841=
      replay15841.length===actualOut15841.length&&
      replay15841.every((candidate15841,index15841)=>candidate15841===actualOut15841[index15841]);
    fate15841.levels.push({
      levelIndex:levelIndex15841,
      elevation:Number(params15841.L),
      consumedSeedBudget:maxSeeds15841,
      sampledEligibleCandidates:found15841.length,
      selectedCount:actualOut15841.length,
      targetSampledEligibleCount:found15841.filter(candidate15841=>inside15841(candidate15841.x,candidate15841.y)).length,
      targetSelectedCount:actualOut15841.filter(candidate15841=>inside15841(candidate15841.x,candidate15841.y)).length,
      targetNotSampledByStrideCount:notSampledTargetCount15841,
      selectionReplayMatchesActual:replayMatchesActual15841
    });
    return fate15841;
  }
  function earthlineFinalizePreselectionFate15841(diag15841){
    const fate15841=diag15841&&diag15841.preselectionFate15841;
    if(!fate15841)return null;
    const counts15841=(fate15841.levels||[]).map(level15841=>Number(level15841.selectedCount||0));
    const sorted15841=counts15841.slice().sort((a15841,b15841)=>a15841-b15841);
    const frequency15841={};
    for(const count15841 of counts15841)frequency15841[count15841]=(frequency15841[count15841]||0)+1;
    const reasonCounts15841={};
    for(const record15841 of fate15841.records||[]){
      const reason15841=record15841.terminalSelectionReason||"unknown";
      reasonCounts15841[reason15841]=(reasonCounts15841[reason15841]||0)+1;
    }
    fate15841.summary={
      levelsObserved:counts15841.length,
      totalSelected:counts15841.reduce((sum15841,value15841)=>sum15841+value15841,0),
      minSelected:sorted15841.length?sorted15841[0]:0,
      maxSelected:sorted15841.length?sorted15841[sorted15841.length-1]:0,
      meanSelected:counts15841.length?counts15841.reduce((sum15841,value15841)=>sum15841+value15841,0)/counts15841.length:0,
      medianSelected:sorted15841.length?(sorted15841.length%2?sorted15841[(sorted15841.length-1)/2]:(sorted15841[sorted15841.length/2-1]+sorted15841[sorted15841.length/2])/2):0,
      selectedCountFrequency:frequency15841,
      levelsAtBudget:counts15841.filter(value15841=>value15841>=Number(fate15841.consumedSeedBudget)).length,
      targetRecordCount:(fate15841.records||[]).length,
      targetSelectedRecordCount:(fate15841.records||[]).filter(record15841=>record15841.selected).length,
      targetReasonCounts:reasonCounts15841,
      selectionReplayAllMatch:(fate15841.levels||[]).every(level15841=>level15841.selectionReplayMatchesActual)
    };
    return fate15841.summary;
  }
  /* BUILD-CHECK-15841-PRESELECTION-FATE-END */

  /* EARTHLINE 16460 — exact membership index for the existing contour seed scan.
     The same static gates and exact four-corner level-crossing test are evaluated
     once per row-major cell. Each contour level then receives the identical ordered
     cell list that the former per-level full-grid rescan would have visited. */
  let earthlineSeedLevelIndex16460=null;
  function earthlineBuildSeedLevelIndex16460(levels,maxS,chanThr,regional){
    const stride=regional?4:2,byLevel=Array.from({length:levels.length},()=>[]),levelMap=new Map();
    for(let li=0;li<levels.length;li++)levelMap.set(levels[li],li);
    const lowerBound=value=>{let lo=0,hi=levels.length;while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(levels[mid]<value)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}return lo;};
    const upperBound=value=>{let lo=0,hi=levels.length;while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(levels[mid]<=value)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}return lo;};
    let staticEligible=0,indexedMemberships=0;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y+=stride)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x+=stride){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS)continue;
      const h00=M.h[i],h10=M.h[idx(x+1,y)],h01=M.h[idx(x,y+1)],h11=M.h[idx(x+1,y+1)];
      const mn=Math.min(h00,h10,h01,h11),mx=Math.max(h00,h10,h01,h11);
      if(!(mx-mn>1e-7))continue;
      staticEligible++;
      const lo=lowerBound(mn),hi=upperBound(mx);
      for(let li=lo;li<hi;li++){byLevel[li].push(i);indexedMemberships++;}
    }
    const built={searchGen:Number(M.searchGen||0),terrainStamp:String(M.terrainStamp||''),maxS:Number(maxS),chanThr:Number(chanThr),regional:!!regional,stride,levels:levels.slice(),levelMap,byLevel,staticEligible,indexedMemberships};
    window.EARTHLINE_SEED_LEVEL_INDEX_16460={build:'EARTHLINE 16460',levels:levels.length,staticEligible,indexedMemberships,stride,at:new Date().toISOString()};
    return built;
  }

  function seedCandidatesForLevel(L,maxS,chanThr,maxSeedsPerLevel){
    const found=[];
    const maxAcc=Math.max(2,M.maxAcc||2);
    const minorThr=Math.max(2,Number(streamThreshold()||2));
    const regional=!!(
      M.loc&&(
        /Regional Opportunity Tile/i.test(String(M.loc.name||""))||
        (Array.isArray(M.loc.zones)&&M.loc.zones.includes("Regional Tile"))
      )
    );
    const stride=regional?4:2;
    const maxSeeds=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(maxSeedsPerLevel)||0));
    let eligibleCrossingCandidates=0;

    const index16460=earthlineSeedLevelIndex16460;
    const indexed16460=!!(index16460&&Number(index16460.searchGen)===Number(M.searchGen||0)&&index16460.terrainStamp===String(M.terrainStamp||'')&&Number(index16460.maxS)===Number(maxS)&&Number(index16460.chanThr)===Number(chanThr)&&index16460.regional===regional&&index16460.stride===stride&&index16460.levelMap.has(L));
    const visit16460=indexed16460?index16460.byLevel[index16460.levelMap.get(L)]:(()=>{
      const rows=[];
      for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y+=stride)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x+=stride){
        const i=idx(x,y);
        if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
        if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS)continue;
        if(!earthlineCellCrossesLevel(x,y,L))continue;
        rows.push(i);
      }
      return rows;
    })();
    for(const i of visit16460){
      const x=i%GW,y=(i/GW)|0;

      const acc=Math.max(1,Number(M.acc[i]||1));
      const flow=Math.log1p(acc)/Math.log1p(maxAcc);
      const central=1-Math.min(1,Math.hypot(x-GW/2,y-GH/2)/Math.hypot(GW/2,GH/2));

      /* Terrain gradient is normal to the contour. The contour tangent is its
         perpendicular. Compare that tangent with the existing D8 flow vector:
         1.0 means the runoff crosses the contour at 90 degrees; 0.0 means the
         contour runs parallel to the runoff and receives no crossing reward. */
      const gx=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y)]||0)-Number(M.h[idx(x-1,y)]||0);
      const gy=Number(M.h[idx(x,y+1)]||0)-Number(M.h[idx(x,y-1)]||0);
      const gmag=Math.hypot(gx,gy);
      const d=Number(M.dir&&M.dir[i]);
      let crossingGeometry=0;
      if(gmag>1e-9&&d>=0&&d<8){
        const tx=-gy/gmag,ty=gx/gmag;
        const fmag=D8D[d]||1;
        const fx=D8X[d]/fmag,fy=D8Y[d]/fmag;
        crossingGeometry=Math.abs(tx*fy-ty*fx);
      }

      const eligibleMinor=acc>=minorThr&&acc<chanThr&&d>=0;
      const accumulationValue=eligibleMinor
        ?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(Math.log1p(acc)-Math.log1p(minorThr))/Math.max(1e-9,Math.log1p(chanThr)-Math.log1p(minorThr))))
        :0;
      const flowCrossingValue=eligibleMinor
        ?crossingGeometry*(0.55+0.45*accumulationValue)
        :0;
      if(flowCrossingValue>0)eligibleCrossingCandidates++;

      /* Dominant crossing rank, followed by the previous flow/central quality.
         This is lexicographic in effect: any meaningful eligible crossing wins
         over a non-crossing candidate, while existing quality breaks ties. */
      const legacyQuality=flow*.78+central*.22;
      const score=flowCrossingValue*10+legacyQuality;
      found.push({
        x,y,score,
        flowCrossingValue,
        crossingGeometry,
        eligibleMinor,
        accumulation:acc,
        legacyQuality
      });
    }

    found.sort((a,b)=>
      b.flowCrossingValue-a.flowCrossingValue||
      b.legacyQuality-a.legacyQuality||
      b.accumulation-a.accumulation||
      a.y-b.y||a.x-b.x
    );
    const out=[];
    for(const c of found){
      if(out.every(s=>Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y)>(regional?18:24)))out.push(c);
      if(out.length>=maxSeeds)break;
    }

    earthlineObserveSeedPreselection15841({
      L,maxS,chanThr,maxSeeds,regional,stride,maxAcc,minorThr,found,out
    });

    M.flowCrossingScoring15833=M.flowCrossingScoring15833||{
      build:"BUILD-CHECK-15833",
      levelsScored:0,
      candidatesScored:0,
      eligibleCrossingCandidates:0,
      selectedSeeds:0,
      selectedCrossingSeeds:0
    };
    const audit=M.flowCrossingScoring15833;
    audit.levelsScored++;
    audit.candidatesScored+=found.length;
    audit.eligibleCrossingCandidates+=eligibleCrossingCandidates;
    audit.selectedSeeds+=out.length;
    audit.selectedCrossingSeeds+=out.filter(s=>s.flowCrossingValue>0).length;
    audit.lastLevel={
      elevation:Number(L),
      candidates:found.length,
      eligibleCrossingCandidates,
      selected:out.length,
      selectedCrossings:out.filter(s=>s.flowCrossingValue>0).length
    };
    return out;
  }


  /* BUILD-CHECK-15833 — candidate-segment evidence scorer.
     This does not move, rotate, extend, or synthesize geometry. It evaluates the
     already traced contour segment against the frozen D8/accumulation field.
     The on-contour guard is a validity check; only valid segments are ranked. */
  function earthlineOnContourGuard15833(seg,L,levelStep){
    if(!Array.isArray(seg)||seg.length<3)return false;
    const tolerance=Math.max(0.35,Number(levelStep||1)*0.75);
    let checked=0,maxDeviation=0,sumDeviation=0;
    const stride=Math.max(1,Math.floor(seg.length/24));
    for(let p=0;p<seg.length;p+=stride){
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(GW-1,Math.round(seg[p][0])));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(GH-1,Math.round(seg[p][1])));
      const h=Number(M.h[idx(x,y)]);
      if(!Number.isFinite(h))continue;
      const dev=Math.abs(h-Number(L));
      checked++;sumDeviation+=dev;if(dev>maxDeviation)maxDeviation=dev;
    }
    if(!checked)return false;
    return (sumDeviation/checked)<=tolerance&&maxDeviation<=tolerance*2.2;
  }

  /* EARTHLINE 16457 — performance only. Preserve the exact flow-evidence score,
     crossing counts, high-energy counts and sorted path IDs. Cache the cell-local
     terms that do not depend on the contour tangent, use numeric hit keys while
     scoring, and allocate the very large per-hit diagnostic object stream only for
     the one C8 instrumentation candidate that consumes it. No selection input changes. */
  let earthlineFlowEvidenceCellCache16457=null;
  function earthlineSegmentFlowEvidence15833(seg,chanThr,captureEvents15835=false){
    const minorThr=Math.max(2,Number(streamThreshold()||2));
    const maxAcc=Math.max(minorThr+1,Number(M.maxAcc||minorThr+1));
    const cacheKey16457=[Number(M.searchGen||0),minorThr,Number(chanThr),maxAcc,String(M.terrainStamp||'')].join('|');
    if(!earthlineFlowEvidenceCellCache16457||earthlineFlowEvidenceCellCache16457.key!==cacheKey16457){
      const weight=new Float64Array(N);weight.fill(NaN);
      earthlineFlowEvidenceCellCache16457={key:cacheKey16457,weight,high:new Uint8Array(N)};
    }
    const cellCache16457=earthlineFlowEvidenceCellCache16457;
    const logMinor16457=Math.log1p(minorThr),logMax16457=Math.log1p(maxAcc),logRange16457=Math.max(1e-9,logMax16457-logMinor16457);
    let best=0,total=0,hits=0,highEnergyHits=0;
    const pathKeys16457=new Set(),hitEvents15835=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(seg)||seg.length<2){
      return {score:0,bestCrossing:0,crossingHits:0,highEnergyHits:0,pathIds:[],hitEvents15835:[]};
    }
    for(let p=1;p<seg.length;p++){
      const ax=Number(seg[p-1][0]),ay=Number(seg[p-1][1]);
      const bx=Number(seg[p][0]),by=Number(seg[p][1]);
      const sx=bx-ax,sy=by-ay,smag=Math.hypot(sx,sy)||1;
      const tx=sx/smag,ty=sy/smag;
      const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(smag));
      for(let k=0;k<=steps;k++){
        const q=k/steps;
        const sampleX=ax+(bx-ax)*q,sampleY=ay+(by-ay)*q;
        const cx=Math.round(sampleX),cy=Math.round(sampleY);
        for(let oy=-1;oy<=1;oy++)for(let ox=-1;ox<=1;ox++){
          const x=cx+ox,y=cy+oy;
          if(x<1||y<1||x>=GW-1||y>=GH-1)continue;
          const i=idx(x,y),acc=Math.max(1,Number(M.acc[i]||1));
          const d=Number(M.dir&&M.dir[i]);
          if(acc<minorThr||acc>=chanThr||d<0||d>=8)continue;
          const fmag=D8D[d]||1,fx=D8X[d]/fmag,fy=D8Y[d]/fmag;
          const crossing=Math.abs(tx*fy-ty*fx);
          if(crossing<0.35)continue;
          let weight16457=cellCache16457.weight[i];
          let localSlope16457=0,streamPower16457=0,accNorm16457=0;
          if(!Number.isFinite(weight16457)){
            accNorm16457=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(Math.log1p(acc)-logMinor16457)/logRange16457));
            localSlope16457=Math.max(0,Number(slopePct(x,y)||0));
            streamPower16457=Math.log1p(acc*Math.max(0.01,localSlope16457/100));
            weight16457=0.45+0.35*accNorm16457+0.20*Math.min(1,streamPower16457/logMax16457);
            cellCache16457.weight[i]=weight16457;
            if(accNorm16457>=0.75)cellCache16457.high[i]=1;
          }
          const value=crossing*weight16457;
          best=Math.max(best,value);total+=value;hits++;
          if(cellCache16457.high[i])highEnergyHits++;
          pathKeys16457.add(i*8+d);
          if(captureEvents15835){
            if(!localSlope16457){localSlope16457=Math.max(0,Number(slopePct(x,y)||0));streamPower16457=Math.log1p(acc*Math.max(0.01,localSlope16457/100));}
            const hitCellId='d8-'+x+'-'+y+'-'+d;
            hitEvents15835.push({
              eventId:'fh-'+String(hitEvents15835.length+1).padStart(4,'0'),
              segmentIndex:p-1,sampleIndex:k,sampleFraction:q,
              sourceSegmentPoint:[sampleX,sampleY],sampleCell:[cx,cy],
              neighborhoodOffset:[ox,oy],hitCellId,x,y,cellIndex:i,
              accumulation:acc,direction:d,flowVector:[fx,fy],
              contourTangent:[tx,ty],crossingGeometry:crossing,
              localSlopePct:localSlope16457,streamPowerProxy:streamPower16457,value
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const unique=Math.max(1,pathKeys16457.size);
    const score=best*12+Math.min(4,total/Math.max(1,hits))*2+Math.min(3,Math.log1p(unique));
    const pathIds=Array.from(pathKeys16457,key16457=>{
      const d16457=key16457%8,i16457=(key16457-d16457)/8,x16457=i16457%GW,y16457=(i16457/GW)|0;
      return 'd8-'+x16457+'-'+y16457+'-'+d16457;
    }).sort();
    return {score,bestCrossing:best,crossingHits:hits,highEnergyHits,pathIds,hitEvents15835};
  }


  /* BUILD-CHECK-15791 — authoritative canopy precondition.
     The cooperative canopy function added in 15683 was accidentally nested inside
     init(), so the global name still resolved to the older synchronous function,
     which returns undefined. The prescriber interpreted that undefined as failure
     and returned before touching the DEM. Keep the authoritative precondition in
     the same scope as the authoritative prescriber and call it directly. */
  async function earthlineBuildCanopyField15791(myGen=M.searchGen){
    _canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;
    if(!(M.realMode&&M.satCanvas))return true;
    try{
      const small=document.createElement("canvas");small.width=GW;small.height=GH;
      const g=small.getContext("2d",{willReadFrequently:true});
      g.drawImage(M.satCanvas,0,0,GW,GH);
      const id=g.getImageData(0,0,GW,GH).data;
      _satRgbaData=id;_canopyW=GW;_canopyH=GH;
      _canopyData=new Float32Array(N);_impervData=new Float32Array(N);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){
        let p=y*GW*4,q=y*GW;
        for(let x=0;x<GW;x++,p+=4,q++){
          const r=id[p],gg=id[p+1],b=id[p+2];
          const green=gg-(r+b)/2,lum=(r+gg+b)/3;
          let c=0;
          if(lum<95&&green>12)c=0.55+Math.min(0.4,(green-12)/40);
          else if(green>22&&lum<140)c=0.45+Math.min(0.4,(green-22)/40);
          if(lum>160||r>=gg)c=0;
          _canopyData[q]=clamp(c,0,1);
          const mx=Math.max(r,gg,b),mn=Math.min(r,gg,b),sat=mx?(mx-mn)/mx:0;
          let imp=0;
          if(green<14&&c<0.25){
            if(lum>160&&sat<0.20)imp=0.75;
            else if(lum>=55&&lum<=160&&sat<0.18)imp=0.62;
            else if(b>=r&&b>gg-2&&lum>70&&sat<0.32)imp=0.60;
            else if(lum>=50&&lum<=120&&sat<0.22)imp=0.55;
          }
          _impervData[q]=clamp(imp,0,1);
        }
        if((y&7)===7){
          if(earthlineIsStale(myGen))return false;
          await earthlineTaskYield16464();
        }
      }
      return !earthlineIsStale(myGen);
    }catch(e){
      console.warn("[Earthline] BUILD-CHECK-15791 grid-scale canopy classification skipped",e);
      _canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;
      return true;
    }
  }


  /* BUILD-CHECK-15876 — permanent prescription precondition and authoritative Vermont infrastructure gate.
     This is integrated into the 15861G engine. It does not create a second
     prescription engine and it never treats a blank swale source as success. */
  class EarthlinePrescriptionHalt extends Error{
    constructor(code,detail={}){
      super(String(code||"prescription-halted"));
      this.name="EarthlinePrescriptionHalt";
      this.code=String(code||"prescription-halted");
      this.detail=detail||{};
    }
  }

  function earthlineMaskFrameIdentity15870(){

    function segmentCrossesPath15836(seg,pathCells,tol=1,pathMask16320=null){

      const pathSet16288=pathMask16320?null:new Set(pathCells);
      for(let p=1;p<seg.length;p++){

        for(let k=0;k<=steps;k++){
          const q=k/steps,x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*q,y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*q;
          const minX=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(x-tol)),maxX=Math.min(GW-1,Math.floor(x+tol));
          const minY=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(y-tol)),maxY=Math.min(GH-1,Math.floor(y+tol));
          for(let yy=minY;yy<=maxY;yy++)for(let xx=minX;xx<=maxX;xx++){
            const pi16320=idx(xx,yy);
            if(pathMask16320?!!pathMask16320[pi16320]:pathSet16288.has(pi16320))return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    }
